# New HSK 7-9 Vocabulary List — 5,600 New Entries

This is the complete list of **5,600 vocabulary entries first introduced in HSK 7-9** in the November 2025 HSK examination syllabus. Levels 7, 8 and 9 share this advanced band. Combined with HSK 1–6, it brings the total to **11,000 entries**.

**Continue learning → [hsk-tutor.com](https://hsk-tutor.com/vocabulary?utm_source=github&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=hsk-3.0-vocabulary-dataset&utm_content=word_lists_hsk_7_9_top).** The study files below work on their own.

[← All HSK vocabulary lists](../README.md#download-hsk-vocabulary-lists-by-level) · [Printable HSK 7-9 PDF](../pdf/hsk-7-9-vocabulary.pdf?raw=true) · [New entries CSV](../data/by-level/hsk-7-9.csv?raw=true) · [New entries JSON](../data/by-level/hsk-7-9.json?raw=true) · [Cumulative CSV](../data/cumulative/hsk-7-9.csv?raw=true)

The PDF for this advanced band contains the new 5,600 entries only; use the [complete PDF](../pdf/hsk-1-9-complete-vocabulary.pdf?raw=true) to include earlier levels.

**How to read the list:** pinyin is copied from the syllabus. Trailing digits in Chinese entries distinguish homographs. Parenthetical level labels preserve later-level uses. Empty parts of speech remain empty. English definitions are not included.

名 noun · 动 verb · 形 adjective · 副 adverb · 代 pronoun · 数 numeral · 量 measure word · 介 preposition · 连 conjunction · 助 particle · 叹 interjection. [Full data guide](../README.md#data-format).
